00016: function emptyAnalytics() {
00017:   return { top: [], meta: null };
00018: }
00019: 
00020: function safeCacheRef(ref) {
00021:   if (!ref || typeof ref !== "object") {
00022:     return { current: { key: "", value: emptyAnalytics() } };
00023:   }
00024: 
00025:   if (!ref.current || typeof ref.current !== "object") {
00026:     ref.current = { key: "", value: emptyAnalytics() };
00027:   }
00028: 
00029:   return ref;
00030: }
00031: 
00032: function sameSlot(meta, targetYmd, targetHour) {
00033:   const targetY = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
00034:   const targetH = toHourBucket(targetHour || "");
00035: 
00036:   const y = String(meta?.next?.ymd || "").trim();
00037:   const h = toHourBucket(meta?.next?.hour || "");
00038: 
00039:   return isYMD(targetY) && !!targetH && y === targetY && h === targetH;
00040: }
00041: 
00042: function firstFiniteNumber(...values) {
00043:   for (const v of values) {
00044:     const n = Number(v);
00045:     if (Number.isFinite(n)) return n;
00046:   }
00047: 
00048:   return NaN;
00049: }
00050: 
00051: function sanitizeTop3(top) {
00052:   const out = [];
00053:   const seen = new Set();
00054: 
00055:   for (const item of Array.isArray(top) ? top : []) {
00056:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
00057: 
00058:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) continue;
00059: 
00060:     const grupo = Math.trunc(g);
00061:     if (seen.has(grupo)) continue;
00062: 
00063:     const confidence = firstFiniteNumber(
00064:       item?.displayConfidence,
00065:       item?.confidence,
00066:       item?.scoreProb,
00067:       item?.prob,
00068:       0
00069:     );
00070: 
00071:     if (!Number.isFinite(confidence) || confidence < 0) continue;
00072: 
00073:     seen.add(grupo);
00074: 
00075:     out.push({
00076:       ...item,
00077:       grupo,
00078:       displayConfidence: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.displayConfidence))
00079:         ? Number(item.displayConfidence)
00080:         : confidence,
00081:       confidence: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.confidence))
00082:         ? Number(item.confidence)
00083:         : confidence,
00084:       scoreProb: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.scoreProb))
00085:         ? Number(item.scoreProb)
00086:         : confidence,
00087:     });
00088: 
00089:     if (out.length >= 3) break;
00090:   }
00091: 
00092:   return out;

00095: export function computeTop3Analytics({
00096:   rangeDraws,
00097:   baseDrawState,
00098:   analyticsCacheRef,
00099:   lotteryKeySafe,

00101:   rangeInfo,

00121:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast)) || "";
00122:

00124:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);

00133:     !forcedTargetH
00134:   ) {
00135:     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
00136:     cacheRef.current = { key: "", value: empty };

00141:     typeof sanitizeHistoricalDraws === "function"

00143:           draws: rawList,
00144:           lotteryKey,
00145:           baseDraw: drawLast,
00146:         })

00150:     ? historicalList
00151:     : [];

00164:     .map((d) => `${pickDrawYMD(d) || ""}@${toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)) || ""}`)

00001: export async function fallbackBaseSearch({
00002:   getKingResultsByRange,
00003:   findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw,
00004:   addDaysYMD,
00005:   minDate,
00006:   lotteryKey,
00007:   targetYmd,
00008:   targetHourBucket,
00009:   uf,
00010: }) {
00011:   const key = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00012:   const safeUf = String(uf || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00013:   const safeTargetYmd = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
00014:   const safeTargetHour = String(targetHourBucket || "").trim();
00015: 
00016:   if (!safeTargetYmd || !safeTargetHour || !safeUf) {
00017:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00018:   }
00019: 
00020:   const searchFrom =
00021:     String(minDate || "").trim() ||
00022:     (key === "FEDERAL"
00023:       ? addDaysYMD(safeTargetYmd, -240)
00024:       : addDaysYMD(safeTargetYmd, -90));
00025: 
00026:   const hist =
00027:     (await getKingResultsByRange({
00028:       uf: safeUf,
00029:       dateFrom: searchFrom,
00030:       dateTo: safeTargetYmd,
00031:       mode: "detailed",
00032:       readPolicy: "cache",
00033:     })) || [];
00034: 
00035:   return findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
00036:     draws: hist,
00037:     lotteryKey: key,
00038:     targetYmd: safeTargetYmd,
00039:     targetHourBucket: safeTargetHour,
00040:   });
00041: }
00042: 
00043: export async function loadHistoryRange({
00044:   getKingResultsByRange,
00045:   uf,
00046:   dateFrom,
00047:   dateTo,
00048:   readPolicy = "cache",
00049: }) {
00050:   const safeUf = String(uf || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00051:   const safeDateFrom = String(dateFrom || "").trim();
00052:   const safeDateTo = String(dateTo || "").trim();
00053: 
00054:   if (!safeUf || !safeDateFrom || !safeDateTo) return [];
00055: 
00056:   return (
00057:     (await getKingResultsByRange({

00081:   for (const seed of seeds) {
00082:     const clean = String(seed).split("?")[0];
00083:     if (!clean) continue;
00084: 
00085:     out.push(clean);
00086:

00088:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".jpg"));
00089:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".jpeg"));
00090:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".webp"));
00091:     } else if (/\.jpg$/i.test(clean)) {
00092:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".png"));
00093:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".jpeg"));
00094:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".webp"));
00095:     } else if (/\.jpeg$/i.test(clean)) {
00096:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".png"));
00097:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".jpg"));
00098:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".webp"));
00099:     } else if (/\.webp$/i.test(clean)) {
00100:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".png"));
00101:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".jpg"));
00102:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".jpeg"));
00103:     }
00104:   }
00105: 
00106:   return Array.from(new Set(out.filter(Boolean)));
00107: }

00109: function top3ItemMatchesSlot(item, slot) {
00110:   const targetY = safeStr(slot?.targetYmd || "");
00111:   const targetH = toHourBucket(slot?.targetHour || "");
00112: 
00113:   const nextY = safeStr(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
00114:   const nextH = toHourBucket(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
00115: 
00116:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return false;
00117:   if (!isYMD(nextY) || !nextH) return false;
00118: 
00119:   return nextY === targetY && nextH === targetH;
00120: }
00121: 
00122: export function buildTop3TimelineViewModel({
00123:   todayDraws,
00124:   rangeDraws,
00125:   lotteryKeySafe,
00126:   ymdSafe,
00127:   analysisYmd,
00128:   publicBase = "",
00129: }) {
00130:   const day = Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : [];
00131:   const range = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
00132: 
00133:   const safeAnalysisYmd = safeStr(analysisYmd || "");
00134:   const safeYmd = safeStr(ymdSafe || "");

00001: import { safeStr, isYMD, toHourBucket } from "../top3.formatters";
00002: 
00003: import {
00004:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00005:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00006:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00007: } from "../top3.constants";
00008: 
00009: import {
00010:   getScheduleForLottery,
00011:   buildMilharesForGrupo,
00012:   buildTimelineTop3,
00013:   auditTop3Timeline,

00018: function clampProb(v) {
00019:   const n = Number(v);
00020:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
00021:   if (n > 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / 100));
00022:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
00023: }
00024: 
00025: function resolveProbValue(x) {
00026:   return clampProb(x?.scoreProb ?? x?.prob ?? x?.probCond ?? 0);
00027: }
00028: 
00029: function normalizeMilhar4(v) {
00030:   const dig = String(v || "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
00031:   if (!dig) return "";
00032:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
00033: }
00034: 
00035: function build4ColsFromEngineOut(out, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
00036:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(out?.dezenas) ? out.dezenas : [];
00037:   const slots = Array.isArray(out?.slots) ? out.slots : [];
00038:   const cols = [];
00039: 
00040:   for (const dz of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
00041:     const items = slots
00042:       .filter((s) => String(s?.dezena || "") === String(dz))
00043:       .map((s) => normalizeMilhar4(s?.milhar))
00044:       .map((m) => (/^\d{4}$/.test(m) ? m : ""))
00045:       .slice(0, perCol);
00046: 
00047:     while (items.length < perCol) items.push("");
00048:     cols.push({ dezena: dz, items });
00049:   }
00050: 
00051:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
00052:     cols.push({ dezena: "", items: Array(perCol).fill("") });
00053:   }
00054: 
00055:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
00056: }
00057: 
00058: function normalizeImgSrc(src, publicBase = "") {
00059:   const s = String(src || "").trim();
00060:   if (!s) return "";
00061:   if (/^(https?:)?\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
00062:   if (/^(data:|blob:)/i.test(s)) return s;
00063: 
00064:   const base = String(publicBase || "").trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");
00065: 
00066:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
00067:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
00068:   return `${base}/${s}`;
00069: }
00070: 
00071: function buildResultStyleImgVariants(grupo, publicBase = "", size = 96) {
00072:   const g = Number(grupo);

00109: function top3ItemMatchesSlot(item, slot) {
00110:   const targetY = safeStr(slot?.targetYmd || "");
00111:   const targetH = toHourBucket(slot?.targetHour || "");
00112: 
00113:   const nextY = safeStr(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
00114:   const nextH = toHourBucket(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
00115: 
00116:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return false;
00117:   if (!isYMD(nextY) || !nextH) return false;
00118: 
00119:   return nextY === targetY && nextH === targetH;
00120: }

00009: /*
00010: TOP3+ — Cadeia de Bichos
00011: Modelo de transição de grupos
00012: 
00013: - Entende targetYmd / targetHourBucket
00014: - Encontra o draw imediatamente anterior ao alvo
00015: - Separa transições por faixa de horário
00016: - Evita ordenar sorteio sem hora como 00h

00020: function safeInt(v, fallback = 0) {
00021:   const n = Number(v);
00022:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
00023: }
00024: 
00025: function drawSortTs(draw) {
00026:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00027:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00028: 
00029:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00030: 
00031:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00032:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00033: 
00034:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
00035:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
00036: 
00037:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
00038: }
00039: 
00040: function slotTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
00041:   const safeYmd = String(ymd || "").trim();
00042:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
00043: 
00044:   if (!isYMD(safeYmd) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00045: 
00046:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00047:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00048: 
00049:   const [Y, M, D] = safeYmd.split("-").map(Number);
00050:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
00051: 
00052:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
00053: }
00054: 
00055: function dedupeAndSortDraws(draws) {
00056:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00057:   const map = new Map();
00058: 
00059:   for (const d of list) {
00060:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);

00063: 
00064:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
00065: 
00066:     const key =
00067:       String(d?.id || d?.drawId || "").trim() ||
00068:       (isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "");
00069: 
00070:     if (!key) continue;
00071: 
00072:     const prev = map.get(key);
00073:

00082: function makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour) {

00084:   const to = toHourBucket(toHour);
00085:

00108: export function computeChainTransitions(draws) {
00109:   const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
00110:   const transitionsAny = new Map();
00111:   const transitionsByEdge = new Map();
00112: 
00113:   for (let i = 0; i < list.length - 1; i += 1) {
00114:     const fromDraw = list[i];
00115:     const toDraw = list[i + 1];
00116: 
00117:     const fromGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(fromDraw));
00118:     const toGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(toDraw));
00119:     const fromHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(fromDraw));
00120:     const toHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(toDraw));
00121: 
00122:     if (!Number.isFinite(fromGrupo) || !Number.isFinite(toGrupo)) continue;
00123:     if (fromGrupo < 1 || fromGrupo > 25) continue;
00124:     if (toGrupo < 1 || toGrupo > 25) continue;
00125:     if (!fromHour || !toHour) continue;
00126: 
00127:     const anyRow = getNestedMap(transitionsAny, fromGrupo);
00128:     anyRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(anyRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
00129: 
00130:     const edgeKey = makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour);
00131:     if (!edgeKey) continue;
00132: 
00133:     const edgeMap = getNestedMap(transitionsByEdge, edgeKey);
00134:     const edgeRow = getNestedMap(edgeMap, fromGrupo);
00135:     edgeRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(edgeRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
00136:   }
00137: 
00138:   return { transitionsAny, transitionsByEdge };
00139: }

00164: function rankTransitionMap(rowMap, basedOnGrupo, topN) {
00165:   if (!rowMap || !rowMap.size) return [];
00166: 
00167:   const total = Array.from(rowMap.values()).reduce(
00168:     (acc, n) => acc + safeInt(n, 0),
00169:     0
00170:   );
00171: 
00172:   if (total <= 0) return [];
00173: 
00174:   return Array.from(rowMap.entries())
00175:     .sort((a, b) => {
00176:       if (Number(b[1]) !== Number(a[1])) return Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1]);
00177:       return Number(a[0]) - Number(b[0]);
00178:     })
00179:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, safeInt(topN, 3)))
00180:     .map(([grupo, freq], i) => {
00181:       const prob = Number(freq) / total;
00182: 
00183:       return {
00184:         rank: i + 1,
00185:         grupo: Number(grupo),
00186:         freq: Number(freq),
00187:         prob,
00188:         probPct: Number((prob * 100).toFixed(2)),
00189:         basedOnGrupo: Number(basedOnGrupo),
00190:         totalTransitionsFromGrupo: total,
00191:       };
00192:     });
00193: }

00195: export function predictNextGrupoFromChain(arg1, arg2 = 3) {
00196:   if (Array.isArray(arg1)) {
00197:     const draws = arg1;
00198:     const topN = arg2;
00199: 
00200:     const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
00201:     if (!list.length) return [];
00202: 
00203:     const { transitionsAny } = computeChainTransitions(list);
00204:     const lastDraw = list[list.length - 1];
00205:     const lastGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(lastDraw));
00206: 
00207:     if (!Number.isFinite(lastGrupo) || lastGrupo < 1 || lastGrupo > 25) {
00208:       return [];
00209:     }
00210: 
00211:     const rowMap = transitionsAny.get(lastGrupo);
00212:     const ranked = rankTransitionMap(rowMap, lastGrupo, topN);
00213: 
00214:     return ranked.map((x) => ({
00215:       ...x,
00216:       mode: "legacy_any",
00217:       basedOnHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(lastDraw)),
00218:       basedOnYmd: pickDrawYMD(lastDraw),
00219:       targetHour: "",
00220:       targetYmd: "",
00221:       transitionType: "ANY",
00222:       transitionEdge: "",
00223:     }));
00224:   }
00225: 
00226:   const { draws, targetYmd, targetHourBucket, topN = 3 } = arg1 || {};
00227: 
00228:   const targetHour = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
00229:   const safeTargetYmd = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
00230: 
00231:   if (!isYMD(safeTargetYmd) || !targetHour) return [];
00232: 
00233:   const historyBeforeTarget = getHistoryBeforeTarget(

01078: /* =========================
01079:    Motor principal V1
01080: ========================= */
01081: 
01082: export function computeConditionalNextTop3({
01083:   lotteryKey,
01084:   drawsRange,
01085:   drawLast,
01086:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01087:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01088:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01089:   topN = 3,
01090:   targetYmdOverride = "",
01091:   targetHourOverride = "",
01092: }) {
01093:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
01094:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
01095:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01096:   }
01097: 
01098:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01099: 
01100:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
01101:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
01102:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
01103: 
01104:   if (
01105:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
01106:     !lastH ||
01107:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
01108:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
01109:   ) {
01110:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01111:   }
01112: 
01113:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
01114:     lotteryKey,
01115:     ymd: lastY,
01116:     hourBucket: lastH,
01117:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01118:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01119:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01120:   });
01121: 
01122:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
01123:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
01124: 
01125:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
01126:     ? forcedTargetY
01127:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
01128: 
01129:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
01130:     ? forcedTargetH
01131:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
01132:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
01133:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
01134:   const transition = `${lastH}->${targetH}`;
01135: 
01136:   const useFirstFocusedRanking =
01137:     key !== "FEDERAL" &&

02858: 
02859: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
02860:   const out = new Map();
02861:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
02862:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
02863:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
02864:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
02865: 
02866:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02867:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
02868:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
02869:   }
02870: 
02871:   return out;
02872: }
02873: 
02874: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
02875:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
02876:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
02877:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
02878: }

03494:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
03495: ========================= */
03496: 
03497: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
03498:   const g = Number(grupo2);
03499:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
03500: 
03501:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
03502:   const out = [];
03503: 
03504:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
03505:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
03506:   }
03507: 
03508:   return out.filter(Boolean);
03509: }
03510: 
03511: 
03512: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
03513:   rangeDraws,
03514:   analysisHourBucket,
03515:   schedule,
03516:   grupo2,
03517:   count = 20,
03518:   targetYmd = "",
03519: }) {
03520:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
03521:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
03522:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
03523:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
03524:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
03525: 
03526:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
03527:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
03528:     : 20;
03529: 
03530:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
03531: 
03532:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
03533:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03534:   }
03535: 
03536:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
03537:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03538: 
03539:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
03540:   const byMilhar = new Map();
03541: 
03542:   for (const d of list) {
03543:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03544:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03545:     if (!h) continue;
03546: 
03547:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
03548:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
03549:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03550:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
03551: 
03552:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
03553: 
03554:     for (const prize of ps) {
03555:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
03556:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
03557: 
03558:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
03559:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
03560: 
03561:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03562:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
03563: 
03564:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
03565:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
03566: 
03567:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
03568: 
03569:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
03570:         milhar: m4,
03571:         dezena: dz,
03572:         centena: c3,
03573:         freq: 0,
03574:         score: 0,
03575:         targetHits: 0,
03576:         scheduleHits: 0,
03577:         sameDowHits: 0,
03578:         firstPrizeHits: 0,

03945:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];

03991:   const timeline = [];

03934: function buildTimelineForDate({
03935:   ymd,
03936:   drawsToday,
03937:   drawsRange,
03938:   lotteryKey,
03939:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03940:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03941:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03942: }) {
03943:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
03944:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
03945:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
03946: 
03947:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
03948: 
03949:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
03950:     lotteryKey,
03951:     ymd: targetYmd,
03952:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03953:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03954:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03955:   });
03956: 
03957:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
03958:     .map(toHourBucket)
03959:     .filter(Boolean);
03960: 
03961:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
03962: 
03963:   const daySorted = day
03964:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
03965:     .sort((a, b) => {
03966:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
03967:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
03968:       return ta - tb;
03969:     });
03970: 
03971:   const rangeSorted = range
03972:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
03973:     .sort((a, b) => {
03974:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
03975:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
03976:       return ta - tb;
03977:     });
03978: 
03979:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
03980: 
03981:   for (const d of daySorted) {
03982:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03983:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03984:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
03985:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
03986:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
03987:   }
03988:

03991:   const timeline = [];

03993:

03996:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
03997:

02858: 
02859: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
02860:   const out = new Map();
02861:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
02862:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
02863:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
02864:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
02865: 
02866:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02867:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
02868:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
02869:   }
02870: 
02871:   return out;
02872: }
02873: 
02874: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
02875:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
02876:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
02877:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
02878: }

02880: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
02881:   lotteryKey,
02882:   drawsRange,
02883:   drawLast,
02884:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02885:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02886:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02887:   topN = 3,
02888:   targetYmdOverride = "",
02889:   targetHourOverride = "",
02890: }) {
02891:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : []);
02892:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
02893: 
02894:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02895: 
02896:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
02897:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));

02900:   if (
02901:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
02902:     !lastH ||
02903:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
02904:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
02905:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K

02915:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02916:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02917:   });
02918: 
02919:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
02920:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
02921: 
02922:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02923:     ? forcedTargetY
02924:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
02925: 
02926:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02927:     ? forcedTargetH
02928:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
02929:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
02930:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
02931:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
02932: 
02933:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
02934:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02935:   }
02936: 
02937:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
02938:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
02939:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
02940:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
02941:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
02942:   });
02943: 
02944:   const layers = {
02945:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },

02948:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },

03497: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
03498:   const g = Number(grupo2);
03499:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
03500: 
03501:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
03502:   const out = [];
03503: 
03504:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
03505:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
03506:   }
03507: 
03508:   return out.filter(Boolean);
03509: }

03512: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
03513:   rangeDraws,
03514:   analysisHourBucket,
03515:   schedule,
03516:   grupo2,
03517:   count = 20,
03518:   targetYmd = "",
03519: }) {
03520:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
03521:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
03522:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
03523:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
03524:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
03525: 
03526:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
03527:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
03528:     : 20;
03529: 
03530:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
03531: 
03532:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
03533:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03534:   }
03535: 
03536:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
03537:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03538: 
03539:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
03540:   const byMilhar = new Map();
03541: 
03542:   for (const d of list) {
03543:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03544:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03545:     if (!h) continue;
03546: 
03547:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
03548:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
03549:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03550:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
03551: 
03552:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
03553: 
03554:     for (const prize of ps) {
03555:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
03556:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
03557: 
03558:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
03559:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
03560: 
03561:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03562:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
03563: 
03564:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
03565:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
03566: 
03567:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
03568: 
03569:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
03570:         milhar: m4,
03571:         dezena: dz,
03572:         centena: c3,
03573:         freq: 0,
03574:         score: 0,
03575:         targetHits: 0,
03576:         scheduleHits: 0,
03577:         sameDowHits: 0,
03578:         firstPrizeHits: 0,

04041:           return tb - ta;

04108:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
04109:       drawLast: baseDraw,
04110:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
04111:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04112:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04113:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04114:       topN: 3,
04115:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
04116:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
04117:     });
04118: 
04119: 
04120:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
04121:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
04122:     const computedForSlot =
04123:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
04124: 
04125:     const top3 =
04126:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
04127:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
04128:         : [];
04129: 
04130:     const resultGrupo = currentDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(currentDraw) : null;
04131: 
04132:     const hit =
04133:       Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) && top3.length
04134:         ? top3.some((t) => Number(t?.grupo) === Number(resultGrupo))
04135:         : null;
04136: 
04137:     timeline.push({
04138:       targetYmd,
04139:       targetHour: slotHour,
04140:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
04141:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
04142:       top3,
04143:       resultGrupo: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? Number(resultGrupo) : null,
04144:       hit,
04145:       status: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? "validated" : "pending",
04146:     });
04147: 
04148:     if (currentDraw) {
04149:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
04150:     } else {
04151:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
04152:     }
04153:   }
04154: 
04155:   return timeline;
04156: 
04157: }

03839: 
03840: export function auditTop3Timeline({
03841:   timeline,
03842:   lotteryKey = "",
03843: }) {
03844:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
03845: 
03846:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
03847:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
03848:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
03849:   });
03850: 
03851:   const total = validated.length;
03852: 
03853:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03854:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03855:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03856:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
03857:   }).length;
03858: 
03859:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03860:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03861:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03862:       .slice(0, 3)
03863:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
03864:   }).length;
03865: 
03866:   function pct(n, d) {
03867:     if (!d) return 0;
03868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
03869:   }
03870: 
03871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
03872:     const map = new Map();
03873: 
03874:     for (const slot of validated) {
03875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
03876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
03877:         key,
03878:         total: 0,
03879:         top1Hits: 0,
03880:         top3Hits: 0,
03881:       };
03882: 
03883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
03886: 
03887:       current.total += 1;
03888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
03889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
03890: 
03891:       map.set(key, current);
03892:     }
03893: 
03894:     return Array.from(map.values())
03895:       .map((x) => ({

03864:   }).length;

03866:   function pct(n, d) {
03867:     if (!d) return 0;
03868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
03869:   }
03870: 
03871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
03872:     const map = new Map();
03873: 
03874:     for (const slot of validated) {
03875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
03876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
03877:         key,
03878:         total: 0,
03879:         top1Hits: 0,
03880:         top3Hits: 0,
03881:       };
03882: 
03883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
03886: 
03887:       current.total += 1;
03888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
03889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
03890: 
03891:       map.set(key, current);
03892:     }
03893: 
03894:     return Array.from(map.values())
03895:       .map((x) => ({
03896:         ...x,
03897:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
03898:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
03899:       }))
03900:       .sort((a, b) => {
03901:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
03902:         return b.total - a.total;
03903:       });
03904:   }

LINHA    : 3934
PADRÃO   : timeline
========================================================================================================================
03904:   }
03905: 
03906:   return {
03907:     lotteryKey,
03908:     total,
03909:     top1Hits,
03910:     top3Hits,
03911:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
03912:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
03913:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
03914:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
03915:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
03916:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
03917:       hour: slot.targetHour,
03918:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
03919:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
03920:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
03921:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03922:         .slice(0, 3)
03923:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
03924:       top1Hit:
03925:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
03926:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03927:         .slice(0, 3)
03928:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
03929:     })),
03930:   };
03931: }

02966: 
02967: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
02968:   const out = new Map();
02969:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
02970:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
02971:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
02972:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
02973: 
02974:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02975:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
02976:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
02977:   }
02978: 
02979:   return out;
02980: }
02981: 
02982: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
02983:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
02984:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
02985:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
02986: }

03603: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
03604:   const g = Number(grupo2);
03605:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
03606: 
03607:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
03608:   const out = [];
03609: 
03610:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
03611:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
03612:   }
03613: 
03614:   return out.filter(Boolean);
03615: }

03603: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
03604:   const g = Number(grupo2);
03605:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
03606: 
03607:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
03608:   const out = [];
03609: 
03610:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
03611:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
03612:   }
03613: 
03614:   return out.filter(Boolean);
03615: }

03618: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
03619:   rangeDraws,
03620:   analysisHourBucket,
03621:   schedule,
03622:   grupo2,
03623:   count = 20,
03624:   targetYmd = "",
03625: }) {
03626:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
03627:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
03628:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
03629:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
03630:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
03631: 
03632:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
03633:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
03634:     : 20;
03635: 
03636:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
03637: 
03638:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
03639:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03640:   }
03641: 
03642:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
03643:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03644: 
03645:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
03646:   const byMilhar = new Map();
03647: 
03648:   for (const d of list) {
03649:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03650:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03651:     if (!h) continue;
03652: 
03653:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
03654:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
03655:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03656:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
03657: 
03658:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
03659: 
03660:     for (const prize of ps) {
03661:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
03662:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
03663: 
03664:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
03665:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
03666: 
03667:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03668:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
03669: 
03670:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
03671:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
03672: 
03673:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
03674: 
03675:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
03676:         milhar: m4,
03677:         dezena: dz,
03678:         centena: c3,
03679:         freq: 0,
03680:         score: 0,
03681:         targetHits: 0,
03682:         scheduleHits: 0,
03683:         sameDowHits: 0,
03684:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
03685:         lastTs: 0,
03686:       };
03687: 
03688:       let inc = 1;
03689:
